The <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span> is that each face has probability <span style="display: inline-block; white-space: nowrap; line-height: 1.8;"><sup style="font-size: 90%;">1</sup>&frasl;<sub style="font-size: 90%;">6</sub></span> on every roll.</p><p>Which calculation gives the contribution of face 6 to the <span style='color: #935d00;'>chi-square statistic (&chi;<sup>2</sup>)</span>?</p>	<span style="display: inline-block;
